(5) **Remove the deprecated top-right buttons** on the Sleep page (the global Sleep/Upload pair duplicates *Consolidate now* and Feed's `+`; keep only `?`), and audit the same toolbar on the other pages. **Rails:** no new endpoint reads the bank at capture time; the study list is a projection of the episode queue (same ETag domain); the mascot state machine stays the single source (`BookwormState`), extended not forked. → extends **G107** (sprites), **G124** (where the breakdowns land), **G114** (queue metadata: `origin`, `harness`, `processed_by`); relates **G117** (first Sleep is onboarding step 3). **Shipped 2026-09-05 (`feat/study-desk`, PR #55):** all five redesign points landed — the `reading` mascot state and clock-free bubble (R2/R8), the book pile as the one volume encoding (R1/R9), the study list replacing `SleepQueueCard`/`SleepDebtBreakdown` with its pure logic renamed and re-tested (R11), server-parsed consolidation history with a per-cycle detail and telemetry-joined duration (R4/R5), all four schedule modes (R6/R7), and the deprecated top-right Sleep/Upload pair removed from this page (R10). **Open remainder, not this row's scope:** the same toolbar audit on Graph/Clusters, and the engine/model picker card (**G122**). **v3 (2026-09-05 evening) — the study room** (`feat/study-room`, round-2 design Track A: spec rulings **R-A1…R-A16** binding as written, plan rulings **P1…P20** where the spec left a choice; nine commits, both suites green throughout). **What shipped.** The page is two columns above 1000 pt of content width and the same single 760 pt column below (`sleepLayout(width:)`, pure and naming its own boundary) — left: the hero card, the stage strip and *In the queue*; right: *Memory sources* and *Recent consolidations*. **The room** (R-A2) is five 24×24 props — night window, cushion, mug, plant, desk lamp — plus the worm and the *real* `BookPileView`, composed by one pure `deskSceneLayout(pointSize:uiScale:)` on a single cell lattice at the worm's own snapped size, every prop placed by an integer cell count so `uiScale` enters the picture exactly once (**P12**: two pixel scales in one drawing read as a bug at any zoom, and one snapping call is G130 R6). A new `PixelRenderer` holds the generalized body (`image(grid:gridSize:pointSize:palette:)`, `snappedPointSize`, `nsColors`) with `BookwormRenderer` kept as a thin facade — all four existing `Bookworm*Tests` files pass with **no edits**, which is the proof it forwards rather than re-implements — over its own `NSLock`-guarded `sceneCache` (**P13**: the mascot cache wipes wholesale past 512 entries, so routing scene layers and stage icons through it would make the always-animating worm collateral damage of every page render) and a separate `DeskPalette` whose keys are **disjoint** from the mascot's nine, so a grid can never be drawn with the wrong palette and come out looking *almost* right. The lamp is the room's ONE data-driven bit — lit iff `ScheduleConfig.mode != "manual"`, the same field the queue card's schedule sentence reads, so the art can never disagree with the words: art encodes **state, never quantity**, and every art bit has a text twin (R-A3/**P11**). The nightcap is **baked into** the `sleeping` and `reading` frames only (**P14** — the mascot ruling forbids an accessory layer a consumer has to merge, and a cap that is a function of the state needs no new cache dimension). Geometry deviation, pinned by assertion: the lattice is 90 × 28 cells, not the sketch's 40 × 24 — 24 rows cannot hold a 24-cell worm raised onto a cushion and 40 columns cannot hold both the room and a pile column wide enough for a full-width `BookPileView` spine. **The hero** promotes the count out of the bracket line: `heroCount` / `heroQualifier` / `bracketTail` are three pure functions over one switch and `sleepDebtBracketText` is **re-composed** from them, so every asserted bracket string passes byte-for-byte and the line survives as the hero group's VoiceOver label (**P8**); `first run` is a real qualifier ranked ahead of `behind`/`overdue` (**P9** — nothing has ever been consolidated in that bank, so calling the queue a backlog would be wrong); the 24-block meter never renders without its noun — `Rested n%` idle, hidden entirely when there is no baseline (a fabricated 100% is a lie), `Read a of b` running, with the label's two numbers and the bar's fraction from ONE reading of `resolveOriginCounts` rather than a second field (R-A5/**P7**); three tiles are present-tense or measured, every unknown a `—` with a hover reason (R-A14/**P18**), and a test bans "cluster", "insight", "est" and "~" from every tile because **G107**'s estimate deferral is binding. Recorded consequence of **P8** + **P17**: while a cycle runs the mood is `.sleeping`, `heroCount` is `nil` by the asserted string, and the hero deliberately draws no numeral — the running number is the meter's `Read a of b`, and repeating the queue total beside it would draw one number twice. **The strip** (R-A8/**P15**) substitutes into the meter's slot — not a sixth card — replacing `moodDetailLine`'s "Stage N of 5" text and its bare `ProgressView`: five 16×16 icons at 32 pt with the short labels **Read · Sort · Decide · Notice · File**, read from one `SleepStages.all` that the `?` popover also renders (**P16** — the popover copy is a *hoist*, not a rewrite, its titles and details pinned as literals so a later reword fails loudly instead of forking the description). Only **Read** carries a fill, because stages 2–5 have no per-episode unit at all (`sleep_cycle.progress_pct` returns `None` past stage 0) and must not be given a fake one; `status.stage` counts COMPLETED stages and that translation happens in exactly one place; a cancel or a failure **freezes** the strip at the stage it reached — resetting to all-pending would erase the stages that really ran, which is precisely what a person wants to see after cancelling. The pulse period is a named constant (≤ 1.2 s) so R-A13 is assertable rather than a literal inside a view, and Reduce Motion holds the terminal frame. **The queue card** becomes the page's schedule statement: a 3 pt micro-fill behind the count instead of a 60 pt `ProgressView` (no spinner where a real count exists) and a dimmed ✓ at `read == total` — `queueRowState`'s **precedence** is the reason it exists as a pure function, since `total == 0` (a source the episode cap left out) is also `read == total`, so checking `.done` first would draw a finished tick over a source nothing has touched — then the schedule sentence for all four modes, and a footer naming the scheduled engine **only when it differs** from the manual one, so the standing quota ruling is shown rather than silently applied. **The right column** is a read-only projection of `store.sourcesOverview` (R-A10 — no new fetch, no new endpoint): `memorySourceRows` drops sources with no captures, orders by the last 14 UTC days then lifetime captures then id, caps at six, and carries a 30-day sparkline plus four week dots over the new sparse `activity` histogram, dense and oldest-first so a silent day is a zero rather than a gap the view has to guess at; history rows gain the commit's time of day and the **engine·author** pill, where an absent `Cicada-Engine:` trailer drops the pill's left half rather than naming an engine that never ran. **Amendment to R1, stated out loud: one volume encoding *per surface*, and no number drawn twice.** R1 was written as "the book pile is the page's one volume encoding"; v3 adds a second surface with its own quantity, so the rule is restated: the book pile encodes **queued characters per source** on the left, a sparkline encodes **captures per day** on the right, each list keeps its own noun on the row and never only in a tooltip — the queue says `waiting` (and `read of total` while running), Memory sources says `captured` — and no figure appears in two places (**P17**). R1's original refusals stand unchanged (no bars-per-source on the queue, no tiles restating the same number, no age histogram), and **P10** extends them into the art: **no painted books anywhere on the page**. The page's one chart is a pile of books, so nothing that is not the chart may look like one; the scene reserves a column and the real `BookPileView` stands in it, with a test proving no prop's ink reaches into that column at any scale. **Two spec rulings amended, each for a measured reason.** (i) **R-A7** asks for a *tree-wide* "exactly one file defines a Consolidate control"; grepped, `sleepVM.triggerManually()` lives in **five** files — the Sleep folder plus the shared top-bar controls, the Sources queue strip, the onboarding Sleep step and the app entry point — four surfaces that each legitimately own their own trigger, three of them behind another track's ownership fence. So the lint walks `Views/Sleep/` and asserts exactly one file there defines it: a deliberate scoping, not a shortcut, and still a strict upgrade on the old form, which grepped `SleepView.swift` alone and would have stayed green while the control moved to a sibling file. (ii) **R-A9** routes the "Change…" link through `AppRouter`, which has **no section field and cannot open the Settings scene** — the private AppKit selector is accepted and silently ignored on macOS 26, and a build-failing test already bans it. The proven mechanism is `SettingsLink` plus a seeded `cicada.settingsSection`, extracted once as `SettingsSectionLink` so that key has exactly ONE **writer**; the lint asserts one writer and never "one mention", because that form is false today and always will be — the literal legitimately appears in four files (the `@AppStorage` reader plus doc comments). A third amendment came out of review: **R-A12** names the snapshot's `loadedAt` as the staleness chip's date, but that field is stamped on a **disk-cache** hydrate, so a cold launch with the backend stopped printed the minute the app opened over data of any age — the fabricated timestamp the feature exists to refuse. `Snapshot.refreshedAt` was split out to mean "the backend confirmed this" (stamped on a 200 **and** a 304, cleared at every hydrate so a snapshot — possibly a previous bank's — never carries a confirmation it did not get); `loadedAt` keeps its change-token job. **Final review added the threshold R-A12 never had, and one cache bump.** `store.isConnected` means "the SSE stream is open", not "the backend is alive": `SyncEngine.start` holds it false for the whole reconnect backoff (1 s doubling to 30 s) while the loop inside that window keeps polling `GET /sync/version` every 3 s, so the chip fired on every backend restart and printed *Not connected — showing the last reading · as of HH:MM* over a timestamp seconds old: a warning contradicted by its own date, on the one feature here built to be honest about freshness. `SleepLiveness.staleAfter` (60 s, asserted greater than `SyncEngine.maxBackoff`, which is now a `nonisolated static` so the relation is checked rather than copied) is the fourth refusal, and it is what the long-declared `now` parameter was reserved for. Separately, `SourceOverview` gained `activity` without bumping `SnapshotCache.schema`: a hydrate restores the pre-`activity` body **and its etag**, the next refresh takes a 304, and the row rendered a flat sparkline and "0 of the last 4 weeks had captures" beside its own non-zero capture count until an unrelated bank write moved the tag — so the envelope goes to 2. **Ship the ETag, its client mapping and the cache version together**: the disk cache is the third half of that rail, and the docstring on `schema` now says so. **Two traps that cost measurement, recorded as evidence.** (1) `date.fromisoformat` **rejects** a datetime string on CPython 3.12 (measured in the project venv), and two call sites bound the telemetry-ledger read by the very value `--date=iso-strict` was about to change — unsliced, both history endpoints would have 500'd the moment the format moved, so the `[:10]` slice ships in the **same commit** as the format change. (2) `raw[:10]` on an episode timestamp calls a **naive-local** stamp a UTC day: silently off by one for exactly the rows nobody checks, so `_activity_day` parses all three shapes a bank holds (aware `+00:00`, legacy naive-local, `Z`-suffixed imports) instead of slicing. `activity` keys are absolute dates, never a rolling array, so a client holding a 304 across UTC midnight renders a day **short**, never a day **shifted** — the accepted asymmetry, stated in the docstring; the sparkline indexes them on a UTC calendar for the same reason (**P2**). The ETag recipe did not move — `activity` is computed from episodes, a component `/sources/overview` already ETags and `VersionVector` already routes — and that is pinned behaviourally by recomputing the tag, not by grepping the router. **Deliberately not in v3:** a time-of-day sky (art only, and state must outrank the clock), mascot identity (**G127** — the nightcap is the only character-specific art, sequenced last so a flip costs one call), any per-cycle estimate, and any price or token readout (the 2026-09-03 ruling). **v4 (2026-09-23) — the mascot page (Track Z, part a).** Rodrigo 2026-09-23: *"Change the mascot page, make it better and have it be more interactive, keeping the minimal vibes."* Round-3 decision 5 amends v3's inert room; the design panel's rulings **R-Z1…R-Z14** (`docs/specs/2026-09-23-round3-design-mascot-page.md` §3) bind as written, and the plan (`docs/plans/2026-09-23-mascot-page.md`) took **Z-P1…Z-P27** where the design left a choice or contradicted the code; nine task commits (Z0–Z8), both suites green throughout. **What shipped**, by id rather than restated: the one-column page with one sentence, one control, one whisper line and a closed, remembered Details (R-Z5, R-Z6, R-Z7); response art on the one lattice (R-Z1, R-Z4) behind an inert art layer and a tested hotspot layer (R-Z8); spines, the lamp and the whisper line as controls that never change what the machine does behind a label (R-Z2, R-Z9); the completion cheer and "See what changed ›" as the only input-free beats besides the weather crossfade (R-Z12); the window as weather (R-Z11); the sentence and the answer ladder as pure, length-capped lines (R-Z13). **The plan rulings later work must keep:** **Z-P1** — the running stage is `activeStage(completed:)` at every site (page mood, menu bar, onboarding, strip) because the wire counts *completed* stages; **Z-P3** — "the last cycle" is `kind == "sleep"`, never `!= "decay"`, since `/sleep/history` also returns inbox-resolution commits and one of those must never be called a cycle or opened by "See what changed ›"; **Z-P4** — the whisper line took the schedule row with it in one commit, so the schedule is never stated twice and ruling 4 (the scheduled engine) is never off the page; **Z-P13** — the pane art was re-checked against the *real* worm's union over every pose and reaction, per weather, and two clouds (overcast's and storm's second) failed the half-visible rule and moved to the right pane's free corner (fair's second cloud sits exactly on the threshold — re-run `WindowSpritesTests` before touching it or the reading frames); **Z-P10** — the renderer key gains one `look` segment, measured at 216 keys per size against the design's ≤ 256 bound (keying pose × reaction would have blown it); **Z-P24** — the frame memo shipped only because its benchmark halved the time per call (measured 0.514 s → 0.003 s). **Three defects fixed first (Z0):** the stage off-by-one at three sites (`SleepMood`, the menu bar's `BookwormState`, and the onboarding step the design missed) — the worm, the bracket line and VoiceOver said stage 1 while Sort ran; the cancel that digested (any running→idle edge stamped `justFinishedAt`, so a cancel chewed for 6 s); and two invented stage lines that asserted a contradiction and a habit on every cycle, which no cycle measured. **Not in part a, landed in part b (below):** feeding (Z9, once Track I's `IntakeRouter` existed — the poses and reactions it needed were already built and tested) and the Meadow pass with the optional sky band (Z10). **Still open:** live verification with screenshots from the demo bank (Z11, the orchestrator's). **v4 part b (2026-09-23) — feeding, the Meadow pass, two live-check fixes (Track Z Z9/Z10; `docs/plans/2026-09-23-mascot-page-b.md`, rulings Z-B1…Z-B19).** Five task commits, both suites green throughout. **Two live-check defects, fixed first.** *The pile overflowed the room:* spine heights were authored in unscaled points (8…40) with nothing bounding their sum, so a large queue grew the pile upward until the card's clip cut its top spine — four full-height spines need 4 × (40 + 2) = 168 pt of a 130 pt column at 1.0× — while at 0.8× a 150 pt spine was wider than its 120 pt column and at 1.4× the spines stayed put as the room grew 40 % (two scales in one picture). *The title was still SF 20 semibold:* the Sleep header had copied `PageHeader`'s font instead of sharing it, so G137's display serif never reached it. The rule now: **`fitPile` compresses the pile to its column and never cuts it** — natural heights when they fit, otherwise one factor over only what sits above a label floor, so the order (the pile's one comparison) and every count survive (Z-B1); **at most eight spines** (seven + a remainder), because 8 × (floor + gap) fits all seven zoom steps (102.4 of 104 pt at 0.8×, 153.6 of 156 pt at 1.2×, the two tightest) while nine need 144 pt of 130 at 1.0× — `PileFitTests` recomputes every step; heights scale with the lattice's cell and the floor with the font (Z-B2); a full-width spine is exactly the column's width (Z-B3); the title is `PageTitle`, the one view `PageHeader` also draws (Z-B4). **Feeding** (R-Z10, Z-B5…Z-B11, Z-B17…Z-B19), by id: `IntakeRouter.feedGuard` at every `IntakeRouter` door — harness session roots and Cicada's own home refused before anything is walked, a refused root met inside a dropped folder never descended into, both sides compared resolved and component by component (Z-B5), with only a symlink resolved during the walk (final review: resolving every entry on the main actor made a 20,600-entry walk 0.08 s → 0.42 s; links-only measures 0.14 s); the pickers outside the router (the Add-source walkthrough's drop and *Choose file…*, its saved-content picker, Settings' local-folder picker) check the choice itself with `refusedRoot(of:)`, and a watched folder that *contains* a refused root is still walked — open; `accept` answers, so the room says a refusal in the worm's words while every other door shows it in the panel (Z-B6, Z-B18 — neither voice promises a capture that may not be wired, and the design's "feeds and notes" became "feeds and saved links" because the intake does not read a note); the verdict at the drop, never during the hover (Z-B7); the window's veil yielding by an explicit `nearerDrop` claim (Z-B8); the router's own phase as the line, with no digit in any feed line (Z-B9, R-Z3); gulp for yes and shake for no, both through the §6.4 matrix — a sleeping worm takes the drop and stays asleep, a stale page sends nothing (Z-B10); what ends a feed line (Z-B11) — including an import that finishes or fails with its panel closed, which the final review found left a live line over the status for good; the slot ranks a drag, then an answer rung, then the feed line, then the status; one `IntakePicker` for every door (Z-B17); T13 as the happy worm's invitation once the queue has loaded (Z-B19). **Meadow** (Z-B12…Z-B15): the sentence's lead is Instrument Serif 30, shrinking only to the 22 pt display floor, over a New York italic tail (the brief over design §5 — a two-line tail is text); `SleepMotion` forwards its shared names to `CicadaMotion`, the equalities pinned by a test; Consolidate is the page's one `PrimaryActionButton` and Cancel stays quiet; hover acknowledges controls, never art. **The sky band ships OFF** (Z-B16, TODO ruling 10): built behind `SkyBand.ships` and every build gate passed (tint ≤ 1.35:1 against the page, title ≥ 7:1, both modes), but against a no-band control the light-mode night and dusk bands read as a neutral grey haze on the title — the smudge the owner's §16 question named — the dark night band was invisible and dark day a lighter slate strip; only light day read as a tint. Measured tint / title ratios (: 1): light day 1.04 / 14.8, dusk 1.26 / 12.2, night 1.29 / 11.9; dark day 1.29 / 12.4, dusk 1.02 / 15.7, night 1.00 / 16.0. APPLY, M **DS-3b:** Details in list grammar; 'Rested' is a sentence. **2026-09-29:** Sleep page v5 (**G163**, [`2026-09-29-sleep-page-v5-design.md`](../specs/2026-09-29-sleep-page-v5-design.md)) was ruled by the owner as **TODO ruling 13**, which amends R10's "one trigger, one cycle": Consolidate reads everything that is waiting from every door (batches of the configured size, each filed and committed; no sheet and no separate *Read all…*), while a scheduled run reads one batch. Spec Q7 and V5-15 are reversed by that ruling. Ruling 12 (plan figures on Details and the engine menu only) is kept. v6 (2026-10-01, ruling 18): the room is a 160 × 64 lattice at 3 pt per cell at 1.0; the lamp keeps its meaning and gains its fly; P10 holds. **2026-10-02, scenery integrated on the review branch:** R-Z11 now separates clock/manual time, five base weathers from the chosen source and mood overlays. Night or rain darkens the whole room; every worm state has both night lamp sets. Labelled time/weather thumbnails live in Settings → Sleep → The scenery. All 36 sheet pairs, including the inert civil-time wall clock at (94,34), are integrated with the app. Rain's 100 ms holds bring the worst steady-state budget to 1,758 boundaries/minute, including the clock's 60 ticks, under the unchanged 1,800 cap. Settings explicitly pauses room sprites and weather. The room and Settings PNG review is recorded in the art INTEGRATION_REPORT.md. I'd remove the [stack] icon next to the project selector in every page.") | **Implementation review (2026-10-06, dev 3efba432):** Clusters/person cards and picture caches exist. Verify lastReferenced projection/hash, age display and person picture flow; don't reopen audited cache fixes without a reproduction. [Code evidence / acceptance](BACKLOG_IMPLEMENTATION_REVIEW.md#g146). **Earlier reasoning and progress (historical):** **What is there.** Clusters (DS-3c, `app/CicadaApp/Sources/CicadaApp/Views/Clusters/`) is a list of 36 pt rows that carry no logo and no age (`/graph` nodes have no `lastReferenced`); round 4's brief also asked it back toward the earlier icon-rich page without the right-hand percentages. A picture exists only as a G59 logo, and only for `company`/`tool` pages with a guessable domain (`logo_service.py:73` `GUESSABLE_TYPES`, `:278`) — never a person. The entity card's header (DS-3a `EntityDetailCard`) is a `Tag`, words for status and confidence, and the name. The command bar draws a stack glyph before the bank name on every page. **What the owner chose (round-4 decisions 9–11):** Clusters = mock A's icon-led cards; **a picture per entity** — uploaded by hand as a profile picture, and detected for niche things (**G159**); the person card = mock C's top (name, picture, a facts strip) "and everything"; the stack glyph goes, leaving mock B's plain bank name + search. **Constraints:** a manual picture is the person's own act and outranks every detected one; it is a file the person chose, so where it is stored (in the bank, versioned, travels with a handed-over bank — vs. beside the logo cache, which is never in a bank) is this row's decision to make and record; a detected picture is a derived artifact of the outside world and follows the logo cache's rule (`$CICADA_HOME/logos/<bank>/`, never in a bank) and the outbound gates; painted art never sits behind a row or a number (Direction D); rows stay ids-free in telemetry. **Slice 1 shipped (round 4 phase A, T-People; plan `docs/plans/2026-09-24-r4-people.md`).** Decided where the upload lives: **in the bank**, `assets/pictures/<id>.<png\|jpg>`, with `picture: {kind, sha, ext, added}` on the page — the person's own record, versioned and carried by an export; the path derived from the id, never read from the page; `{kind: initials}` keeps "Use initials instead". The app shrinks (ImageIO, ≤ 512 px), the server bounds (PNG/JPEG only, ≤ 512 KB, 16–1024 px, no Pillow). One precedence on both sides over one fixture; `/graph` nodes carry `picture`/`pictureSource` (omitted when absent) and `lastReferenced`. Clusters is A's icon-led cards (F-11); a person's card opens with C's top and has the signed beliefs, the map and What's happening (F-12). The command bar's stack glyph is T-Home's. **Open:** a merged page's picture is not carried to the survivor (the loser's file stays in `assets/pictures/`). **2026-09-30 (G61 S3-b):** the brand rung no longer guesses — a company or tool's logo is its own trusted `website` source's icon (the person's, one they took, or one Cicada's read confirmed), and a page with none keeps its ring monogram; `entity_picture.logo_available` needs a trusted site, an older cache is purged once (`LOGO_RULE` 2). **Previous execution label (historical):** ✅ slice 1. | PARTIAL · P3 |
| G159 | <a id="g159"></a>**Smart entity pictures — a picture for almost anything, the way Revolut shows a logo for almost any merchant** (Rodrigo 2026-09-24: "if possible we should smartly detect a picture for even niche stuff. Revolut does this for almost any payment, im not sure how but maybe research about it.") | **Implementation review (2026-10-06, dev 3efba432):** Trusted-site logos, Contacts photos, media thumbnails and user picks exist. Improve remaining picture coverage only from trusted sources; never guess a person's domain/name or silently fetch their profile image. [Code evidence / acceptance](BACKLOG_IMPLEMENTATION_REVIEW.md#g159). **Earlier reasoning and progress (historical):** **What exists.** **G59** (✅) resolves a logo only for `company`/`tool` pages (`logo_service.py:73,278`), by `logo:` → `sources:` → `## Links` → `media.url` → a `<slug>.com` guess, fetching apple-touch-icon → `<link rel=icon>` → DuckDuckGo's icon service, cached under `$CICADA_HOME/logos/<bank>/` and never in a bank; a `person`, a place, a concept or a book gets a monogram. **The research question — how a bank does it:** a card network passes a raw merchant descriptor; the bank (or an enrichment vendor) cleans it, matches it to a merchant record with a known domain and a curated logo, and falls back to a category icon — a large curated database plus a matching model, not a per-transaction fetch. Cicada has no such database and must not phone one home with the person's entities. **The local-first precedence for Cicada:** (1) a **manual upload** (G146's profile picture — the person's own act, always wins); (2) the **Contacts photo** for a matched person (G154, on this Mac); (3) a **domain logo** (G59's ladder, extended past `company`/`tool` when the page names a domain); (4) a **Wikidata image** (P18/P154 logo) for a page whose identity resolves to a Wikidata item by an exact label + type match — a keyless public API; (5) the **Open Graph image** of a page's own cited URL; (6) the **monogram**. **Constraints:** every rung that reaches the network runs under the existing gates (`CICADA_ALLOW_LOGO_FETCH`, the connector gate for anything Sleep starts unattended) and the ToS rail (4 s / ≤ 512 KB / no cookies / never behind auth), through `net_guard`/`_is_safe_url`; **the lookup sends a name or a domain, never a claim or a sentence**, and never a `person`'s name to a public service (a surname is not a domain — G59's rule, extended: a person gets only rungs 1–2); a guess is shown as a guess until confirmed, and a wrong picture is one click to replace; raster only (SVG refused, as today); cached outside the bank, a manual upload aside (G146 decides where that lives). → extends **G59**; relates **G146**, **G154**, **G70**. **Slice 1 (round 4, T-People):** rungs 1 (the person's upload or initials), 3 (G59's logo, for `company`/`tool` or a page whose `logo:` names a domain — cached, or not yet known to miss) and the media thumbnail ship through `entity_picture.resolve`; rung 2 (Contacts) reads the optional `contacts_photo: {sha, ext}` and `$CICADA_HOME/pictures/<bank>/contacts/<id>.<jpg\|png>`, both T-Sources' to write (G154, its R-SR9 layout). Wikidata P18/P154, Open Library covers and multi-word domain guessing remain this row's RESEARCH. **2026-09-29:** the guessed logo for a page with no URL is recorded as a parked finding in **G173** (its fix belongs to this row's multi-word-domain RESEARCH). The classifier navigator idea (**G168**) never sends a person's name, per this row's rule. **Previous execution label (historical):** 🔲 RESEARCH. | PARTIAL · P3 |
| G23 | <a id="g23"></a>**In-app rich media preview (YouTube playback + thumbnails + hover previews)** | **Implementation review (2026-10-06, dev 3efba432):** Playback and thumbnails exist; the original hover-rich-preview request is not established by those implementations. Restore from DONE; retain the hover acceptance under G25 rather than duplicating a preview system. [Code evidence / acceptance](BACKLOG_IMPLEMENTATION_REVIEW.md#g23). **Earlier reasoning and progress (historical):** In the companion app, opening a video link (or a video markdown) renders the **thumbnail/preview**, and — nice-to-have — **plays the YouTube video inside the app** (WKWebView). **Hovering** a video-link reference shows its thumbnail. → extends **G11** (already ships image lightbox, inline video, website WKWebView); adds in-app YT playback + hover previews. **Previous execution label (historical):** ✅ (2026-07-03 — HeroPreview atop entity pages: YouTube in-app embed player, OG/website hero card, hero image w/ lightbox; reuses MediaPreview/WebView). | PARTIAL · P3 |
| G11 | <a id="g11"></a>**In-app media preview (images · videos · websites) + artifacts as memories** | **Implementation review (2026-10-06, dev 3efba432):** Inline players/previews and saved artifacts exist. Check remaining general preview/provider behavior and the disclosed duplicate direct-file player; playback does not establish video understanding. [Code evidence / acceptance](BACKLOG_IMPLEMENTATION_REVIEW.md#g11). **Earlier reasoning and progress (historical):** Preview rich media **inside the app**: **images** (inline + lightbox), **videos** (inline player / thumbnail-to-play), and **website link previews** (Open-Graph card or a small embedded web view of saved bookmarks/URLs — reuse the M4 media-ingestion OG enrichment for the card; a `WKWebView` for full preview). Let media-as-artifacts be **saved as memories** — an image/video/site embedded in an entity or claim, rendered inline like the transclusion layer renders embedded pages. Extends the inline-transclusion model (`![[…]]`) to media embeds (D2 transclusion currently excludes images/media — revisit) and the M4 `media` entities (which already store `url`/`thumbnail`/`media_type`) gain real in-app previews instead of just opening the URL. Sources: chat-export images (Gemini takeout), saved bookmarks/YouTube/articles, pasted/diagram artifacts. → **underpins G14** (mood-boards are media-preview-heavy); relates to G2 (media types), G3/M4 (feed), the transclusion layer, M5c surfaces. **Preview half shipped 2026-07-03** (G23/G25 — `HeroPreview.swift`/`MediaPreview.swift`/`ImageLightbox.swift`: image inline+lightbox, video inline player, website WKWebView/OG hero card). **Artifacts-as-memories half remains open** — media is not yet embeddable inline inside another entity or claim via the transclusion layer. **Preview half — video (2026-09-05, Track V).** The 2026-07-03 wave played exactly one thing: a YouTube link whose id parsed, inside a `WKWebView`. Now YouTube (including `/live/<id>` and `playlist?list=`), Vimeo, TikTok and Loom play in the provider's own embed player, and a direct `.mp4/.m4v/.mov/.webm/.m3u8` or `file://` clip plays in a real AVKit player with transport controls and space-to-play — an unreadable local path shows the path plus Reveal in Finder, never a black rectangle. **The rule: the provider is DERIVED at read time from the stored URL, never written into a bank** — one classification table written twice (`api/services/video_urls.py` and `Views/Common/VideoRef.swift`) and pinned by one fixture (`api/tests/fixtures/video_urls.json`), pure on both sides, zero network. Two reasons, and the second is the load-bearing one: every already-saved item starts playing the moment the app updates, with no whole-bank rewrite commit; and `GET /sources` reads `media_type` from `url_index.json` while `site`/`channel`/`provider` come from the page's `media:` block, so a write-time reclassification would have to migrate two stores at once — the split-brain class this repo already knows. **Surfaces, all four through the same two components:** the Feed row (a play badge over the thumbnail for a *playable* ref only, plus a duration pill only when a provider reported one; the row is reused by the Sources page), the Feed preview sheet (720 × 560 for the two video kinds, 480 × 520 for everything else), the entity Content tab, and the entity hero (`EmbedVideoHero` / `FileVideoHero`, **never autoplaying** — a hero renders on every visit, not behind a tap). **The ToS rail as it applies here:** only a provider's own player URL is ever loaded; an oEmbed response is read for its *fields* only, never its `html` blob (the player URL is derived from the id we parsed); and **a stream is never derived** — no `yt-dlp`, no CDN or `.m3u8` lifted out of a page — so a direct file the app plays is one the *user* saved as a direct URL. Twitch stays external (its player validates `parent` against the real embedding origin and a top-level `WKWebView` document has none; synthesising one is circumvention), an X `/status/<id>` link stays a website card (it is *any* post, so calling every status a video would be a lie), Instagram stays external (login-walled). Backend is metadata only: a direct file classifies as `media_type: video`, which `link_enrichment._excluded_media` already honours — so the nightly enrichment stops fetching binaries with no edit to that module; one shared `_enrich_oembed` for Vimeo/TikTok/Loom at 4 s / ≤ 512 KB; a content-type guard on the OG fetch; and two additive optional keys (`media.provider`, `media.duration_s`, written only when they have a value, so a plain bookmark's frontmatter is byte-identical to before). **Two named follow-ups, neither attempted here:** (1) teaching `normalize_url`/`_youtube_video_id` about `/live/<id>` changes `url_hash`, so an already-ingested URL would re-import as a *new* entity and orphan its `url_index.json` entry — that is a **dedup-index migration**, not a one-line fix, and the player was fixed at read time instead; (2) **Dailymotion and Reddit embeds are mechanical once the table exists** — but neither endpoint was probed, and adding an unverified endpoint to a ToS-bound path is the wrong trade. **Previous execution label (historical):** 🛠️ preview half shipped; artifacts-as-memories half open. | PARTIAL · P3 |

| G48 | <a id="g48"></a>**Live-conversation provenance + "Recent conversations" resume list** | **Portability follow-up (owner 2026-10-06):** G110 needs stable project/task and source/destination session lineage, not just a Claude Code resume descriptor. Preserve each new session's true harness/model and link its continuation to the source without copying the source session ID or consolidating the same observations twice. Verify project selection and current workspace references through authorized harness observations. Cursor attribution is part of G50/G76. [Continuity acceptance](../specs/2026-10-06-project-continuity.md). **Implementation review (2026-10-06, dev 3efba432):** Session origins and guarded resume paths exist. Complete session/model attribution across supported harnesses and verify recent-conversation resume; no arbitrary transcript reads or desktop Stop-hook claim. [Code evidence / acceptance](BACKLOG_IMPLEMENTATION_REVIEW.md#g48). **Earlier reasoning and progress (historical):** Track which **live conversations** (Claude Code sessions, Cursor chats, any MCP-connected harness) wrote to which part of memory, and surface them as a **"Recent conversations"** list you can **hop back into** (resume). Two parts. **(a) Conversation-level provenance:** today provenance answers *which model* (M3 `Cicada-Author`) and *which harness* (G9 `origin`) but not *which conversation*. Stamp a **`session_id`/conversation id** (+ optional transcript path / resume handle) onto episodes captured live via MCP (`cicada_save_episode`, `cicada_write_claim`), and propagate it episode → entities/claims via `source_episodes` — the exact pattern G9 used to thread `origin`. Claude Code exposes its session id to hooks/MCP context; other harnesses analogously; degrade gracefully when a harness offers none. **(b) Recent-conversations surface:** a recency-sorted list (app view + API endpoint) grouping memory writes by conversation — harness, model, timestamp, and *which entities/claims that conversation touched* — with a one-click **"Resume"** action that reopens the session (`claude --resume <session-id>` for Claude Code; per-harness deep-link where one exists, provenance-only fallback where not). Inverse navigation too: from an entity's history, jump to the conversation that wrote that belief, then back into it live. → extends **G9** (origin: harness → *conversation*), **M3/A2** (contributors: model → *session*); relates to **G12/M7** (imported conversations already carry per-conversation `uuid`s — same grouping applies, minus resume) and the entity history/provenance views. **Rail restated 2026-09-03 (see G105 ruling):** the invariant guards *tool output, code and secrets* — a deterministic block-level extractor may store the person's own turns and the agent's final replies; transcripts are otherwise still never read (the `isfile()` resumability check stands). **Previous execution label (historical):** ✅ (session_id/harness/project_dir stamped at the MCP seam; `Cicada-Session:` trailer; `GET /conversations/recent` + `GET /conversations/{id}` + `POST /conversations/{id}/resume`; Activity ▸ Conversations with Ghostty/Terminal/clipboard Resume; "from conversation" on entity-history + contributor-commit rows. PARTIALS: no backfill — pre-G48 MCP episodes carry no session and don't appear; only Claude Code is resumable (other harnesses group but have no deep link); the inverse-navigation affordance opens the conversation in a popover rather than jumping to Activity ▸ Conversations; `model` on a conversation row is **reserved — always null**, populated once engine calls carry session refs (G49)). | PARTIAL · P3 |
| G49 | <a id="g49"></a>**Subscription-only Cicada — the "Session-Native Engine Ladder"** | **Cursor / continuity follow-up (owner 2026-10-06):** G50 owns adding a supported Cursor provider/engine adapter; this row owns engine/session attribution and ladder behavior. Carry actual source/destination harness and reported model through G110 continuation; never infer a model or reuse another session's identity. Verify supported vendor interfaces before adding a new plan rung; preserve scheduled-plan, cancellation and quota behavior. **Implementation review (2026-10-06, dev 3efba432):** Plan engines and local embeddings exist. Remaining session-native attribution/engine ladder details require actual supported-client checks; the historical no-credits blocker is obsolete. [Code evidence / acceptance](BACKLOG_IMPLEMENTATION_REVIEW.md#g49). **Earlier reasoning and progress (historical):** Adopt the recommended architecture in [`subscription-first-portability.md`](subscription-first-portability.md): a **Claude Code plugin** (SessionStart primer, Stop/SessionEnd auto-capture, PreCompact flush, both skills, MCP registration, new `cicada_commit` tool) with a **Codex CLI mirror** for ChatGPT-plan users; underneath, an **engine ladder** (implement the reserved `llm_mode="agent"`: subscription CLI `claude -p`/`codex exec` → local Ollama → BYOK → skip-with-queue) behind a mandatory `providers.resolve_llm_fn` seam; on top, **keyless onboarding** (plan-picker wizard replacing "paste your key" with vendor OAuth, ungated embedding default removing the HF_TOKEN gate, launchd nightly runner with env-key sanitization + stop-on-throttle). Two non-negotiables all three candidate designs converged on: a deterministic **"structural Sleep"** (decay/hubs/edges/index/inbox/git-commit — zero LLM, runs nightly no matter what, sweep-commits agentic writes) and **bearer-token auth on localhost:8000 as a launch blocker**. Phased P0–P5 (~5–7 solo weeks; Claude-plan user fully served after ~2). Key verified facts: `claude -p` runs on Pro/Max subscription OAuth (billing-split paused Jun 2026); published token-limit figures are **refuted** → budget in invocations + live throttle detection, never tokens. Re-confirm the doc's "NOT verified" checklist (hooks payloads, plugin marketplace, all Codex-side claims) before building each phase. → subsumes the engine half of **G10** (agentic extraction), extends **G9** (origin) + M3 (contributors, per-rung `Cicada-Author`), relates to G13, launch-blocker list, and `install.sh`/onboarding. **Shipped 2026-09-23 (Track E, `feat/engines`) — the engine-ladder half, on both plans.** The `claude-cli` rung hardened the Hermes way, one engine id and no Agent SDK (spec Decision 1): stream-json read for the answer and the CLI's `rate_limit_event`/`system/api_retry`; one pure `plan_limits.claude_stop` stops a cycle before extra usage unless opted in, on any rejected window, and at ≥ 90 % of the 5-hour window, each as one plain sentence with the CLI's own reset time; `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_RETRIES=2`; `--setting-sources ""` as a third capture lock; reasoning-off → `--effort low` (`claude --help` 2.1.280); a Stage-1 `--json-schema` built from the rails, **off** until measured. A `codex` rung (`llm_mode=codex`, `Cicada-Engine: codex-cli`, card `chatgpt-plan`): one `codex exec` per call with the isolation flags verified on codex-cli 0.154.0, per-call instruction files that replace Codex's coding prompt, strict per-stage schemas, and a read-only `codex app-server` pre-flight (signed in, plan vs API key, limit already reached, the plan's default model — no quota). Both rungs share the semaphore, the scoped breaker and the models-used ledger. Auto: Claude plan → ChatGPT plan → Ollama → key; ruling 4 covers both plans through one tuple, `engine_select.SUBSCRIPTION_MODES`. **Measured:** a signed-out `codex exec` emits top-level `error` lines as *retry notices* ("Reconnecting… n/5") before `turn.failed` — only a turn that never completes is a failure (R-E16); one live `claude -p` on 2.1.280 (haiku, `--effort low`, temp home) streamed `system/init` → `assistant` → `rate_limit_event` → `result`, so **a `rate_limit_event` does arrive on a fresh process** (R1 V-S1: yes); `apiKeySource` read `"none"` under the plan sign-in; the event carries **no top-level `utilization`** — each window's fraction sits under `rate_limit_info.unifiedWindows.{five_hour,seven_day}.utilization`, which the reader handles so the 90 % rule can fire at all (sanitised fixture `claude_stream_live.jsonl`); and `--effort low` lowers reasoning without turning it off (the trivial call still emitted thinking); **the isolated home is not free of hidden context** (final review, 2026-09-23: a signed-out 0.154.0 `codex exec` in a throwaway home, pointed at a local capture server with `openai_base_url`, synthetic prompt): Codex unpacks its own bundled system skills into `<home>/skills/.system` and listed them in every request (~10.8k chars) — now off via `-c skills.include_instructions=false`, with `include_permissions_instructions` and `include_collaboration_mode_instructions` for the notes that surface in its place; request body 31.8k → 21.0k bytes. Still riding every call with no working switch: the code-mode/collaboration tool namespace (~15k chars — `exec`, `spawn_agent`… despite `--disable multi_agent`), a `multi_agent_role` note (~3.4k) and `environment_context` (~1k, kept for the date) — so the per-call overhead is roughly R2's ≈ 7.7k tokens *before* the switches, not gone. The signed-in `input_tokens` of one Stage-1 call is **still unmeasured** (needs a signed-in Cicada home) and is owed to the live check. **Fixed on the way:** a model picked in Settings → Sleep never reached a real cycle (`resolve_settings` with no registry dropped it), plan-cycle claims were stamped with `litellm_model` as `authored_by`, and `EngineExhausted` never tripped the breaker. **Ruled out, with the evidence (R2 §3.4):** direct OAuth against `chatgpt.com/backend-api` with Codex's client id. **What stays open:** the plugin / SessionStart / PreCompact half and structural Sleep; flipping `CICADA_AGENT_EXTRACTION_SCHEMA` after a demo-bank comparison; a ChatGPT weekly-ceiling stop (owner DECIDE); MCP `cicada_ask` still resolves from env (Track R moves its body); whether Codex's own `logs_2.sqlite` (or its other sqlite stores) in Cicada's Codex home keeps prompt text — partly checked: after six signed-out `--ephemeral` runs carrying a synthetic sentinel, `logs` held 0 rows, `threads` 0, and the sentinel was in none of the home's sqlite files; a signed-in run (whose reply streams back) is **still unchecked** and owed to the live check with a throwaway signed-in home — if the sentinel appears, point `sqlite_home` at a per-call temp dir or prune after each cycle, since episode text there sits outside the bank and git; the unscoped breaker bucket is never reset after a throttle until the backend restarts, and this track **did** widen it (final review H1, reproduced: R-E23 sent Ask onto the plan, a Codex usage limit raised `EngineThrottled`, and a near-limit stop on a failed call turned any failure into one — one throttle, then every Ask failed fast without spawning) — fixed here by giving each Ask call (`ask:<uuid>`) and each link backfill, tail or on-demand (`links:<uuid>`), its own self-purging scope, and by raising a stop on a failed call only when the plan enforced it (overage, rejected), never for `near_limit`; what still shares `_unscoped` reaches a plan only through an explicit `CICADA_LLM_MODE` (the dedup sweep), fixable with an expiry at the vendor's reset time; a Claude call with no system message and no JSON ask still runs under the CLI's own coding-assistant prompt (live: it refused a plain request as off-role) — the Codex rung has `DEFAULT_INSTRUCTIONS` for that case, the Claude rung no twin yet. **Round 4 (2026-09-24) — the model reservation is lifted for harness writes (owner; TODO ruling 11).** (1) The Stop-hook capture keeps each agent turn's `model`/`effort` in the episode's `turns` sidecar — only `message.model` + the top-level `effort` for Claude Code, `turn_context.payload.model`/`.effort` for Codex, and the hook's `effort.level` for the last reply. (2) Every MCP write, stdio and remote, carries `recorded_ts`. (3) `turn_authorship.py` joins them at read: `authorModel`/`authorEffort` on the claim wire, a model on each assistant span, `models` on a harness contributor, and `agent` plus per-turn models on `/episodes/{id}/text`. (4) `Cicada-Author:` is unchanged. **Known limits:** Codex gives an MCP server no session id, so a Codex MCP write joins to nothing even though its captured turns carry models; the same holds for an app with no hook (the Claude app, ChatGPT, Cursor, a remote connector). No backfill: an episode captured before round 4 gains models only when its session grows again. **Previous execution label (historical):** 🔲. | PARTIAL · P3 |
| G51 | <a id="g51"></a>**Consumption / traceability dashboard — minimal + advanced, GitHub-style calendar, `/stats`-style stats, honest pricing** | **Implementation review (2026-10-06, dev 3efba432):** Cycle, harness and consumption statistics exist with scoped UI. [Code evidence / acceptance](BACKLOG_IMPLEMENTATION_REVIEW.md#g51). **Earlier reasoning and progress (historical):** Append-only JSONL **ledger** at `~/.cicada/telemetry/events-YYYY-MM.jsonl` (never in a bank/git), fed at one seam: `providers.resolve_llm_fn` (timing, `usage`, litellm `response_cost`, list-price `equiv_cost_usd`; **the four remaining direct-litellm callsites — extraction, disambiguation, enrichment, `/ask` — get rerouted through it = G49 P4 seam completion pulled forward**), plus `sleep_run` events in `_finalize` (commit hash, duration, counters) and `agentic_write` events in MCP `cicada_write_claim`; G49 engines emit through the same `record()`. Aggregation merges the ledger with `Cicada-Author` git history (memory writes per day) → `GET /consumption/summary\|calendar\|stats\|connections\|harness`. App: **Usage** page (Provenance) — **minimal**: tiles (cost / memory writes / tokens / streak) + 53-week heatmap with tooltips + day drill-down; **advanced**: per-connection cost cards, Swift Charts (tokens/day, cost/day vs API-equivalent, hour-of-day), by-model/stage/bank tables, `/stats`-style facts (lifetime tokens, favorite model, peak day, longest sleep run), optional harness panel from `~/.claude/stats-cache.json` + newest Codex `rate_limits` snapshot (labelled as the harness's own data). **Honesty rules:** subscription = flat `$/mo` + "≈ $X at API list price — estimate, not billed"; usage = real per-model $; local = free; no Claude rate-limit % (no compliant source) — throttle events observed by Cicada instead; no invented token budgets (G49).
